The company describes it as an \"AI-driven\" RCM solution designed to help healthcare practices \"collect faster, cut denials, and stay compliant.\"\n\n## Core Services Included\n\nThe RCM service encompasses the complete patient billing journey:\n\n1. **Credentialing** - Provider enrollment and verification\n2. **Registration** - Patient intake and demographic capture\n3. **Prior Authorization & Eligibility** - Insurance verification and authorization tracking\n4. **Charge Entry & Coding Review** - Claim preparation and clinical coding validation\n5. **Claim Scrubbing & Submission** - Quality assurance and electronic claim transmission\n6. **Denial & Appeals Management** - Automated tracking and resubmission within 48 hours\n7. **Payment Posting & Patient Collections** - Reconciliation and patient balance management\n8. **Weekly Performance Review** - Ongoing monitoring and optimization\n\n## Staffing Model\n\nAccording to the content, each client receives a dedicated \"MedicsRCM Services Manager experienced in your specialty(ies)\" supported by:\n\n- Dedicated Account Manager\n- Billing Team Members\n- EDI specialists\n- HL-7 interface specialist\n- Senior ADS management team member\n\nThe company operates a team of \"300+ billing and workflow professionals\" handling backend operations.\n\n## How It Works\n\n**Transition Timeline:** Claims submission begins within 30 days after a 5-7 day testing period using 50-100 test claims.\n\n**Data Handling:** Current databases (referring physicians, insurance, fees) are imported into their MedicsPremier practice management system.\n\n**Access & Transparency:** Clients receive 24/7 cloud-based access to all data through their MedicsPremier platform, with customizable reporting exportable to Excel.\n\n## Performance Metrics\n\nThe service targets these benchmarks:\n\n- ~96% average collection rate\n- 99% clean claim rate (first pass submission)\n- <5% denial rate\n- <30 days accounts receivable\n- 90% collections within 90 days\n\n## Pricing Structure\n\nCosts typically range from 3-6% of collected amounts, though this may offset existing technology and staffing expenses.\n\n## Software Integration\n\nMedicsRCM connects with virtually any EHR, hospital system, practice management platform, G/L system, PACS, or laboratory information system. Clients have three EHR options:\n\n1. Interface existing EHR with MedicsPremier\n2. Use MedicsCloud EHR (usually no additional cost)\n3. Use electronic superbill without EHR integration\n\n## Additional Patient-Facing Tools\n\nThe platform includes optional features for patient engagement:\n\n- Telemedicine capabilities\n- Patient portal access\n- Text message reminders\n- Kiosk-based intake\n- Remote patient monitoring (RPM)\n- Patient responsibility estimator\n- Mobile payment options\n\n## Compliance & Security\n\nThe service maintains:\n- HIPAA compliance\n- 99.9% uptime\n- SOC-1 & SOC-2 certifications\n- Disaster recovery protocols"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01VWdkEBUhZzUYf3sGpo4Wwh","session_id":"23f94360-d7fe-400c-8294-9ace18779e06","uuid":"d79aed06-ae19-4776-9eec-cf4db081eb06"}

Company was founded from their NYC apartment.\n- **Current status:** Independent, privately-owned, debt-free company. Never changed name, never involved in acquisitions or mergers, never discontinued a product.\n- **Employees:** Over 300 and growing\n- **Facilities:** Own 15,000-square-foot ADS Building\n- **Internal capabilities:** Own programmers, implementations, training, support, and IT departments -- all in-house\n- **Leadership:** David Barzillai (Founder & President), George Grodentzik (Partner EVP), Sheryl Miller MBA (EVP Sales & Business Development)\n- **Performance metrics:** 45+ years in business, 99% clean claims on first submission rate\n\n---\n\n### SOURCE 3: https://www.adsc.com/history\n\n**Source type:** Vendor timeline/history page\n\n**Key findings (chronological):**\n- **1977:** Created MEDICS I -- a patient demographics database with some billing and claim printing\n- **1979:** MEDICS I updated. Raymond Dimadian MD's radiology practice (where MRI was invented) implements the system; that practice evolved into HMCA (now 25 radiology centers)\n- **1983:** MEDICS II with floppy disk billing and automated scheduling. Reached 500 clients.\n- **1989:** Electronic claims submission via modem; IBM Business Partner\n- **1993:** MedicsElite released (GUI product). Reached 65+ employees, 1,000+ clients\n- **1996:** MedicsDocAssistant EMR limited beta release -- this is the product origin\n- **2000:** MedicsDocAssistant EHR V4 moves to full production. Transitions from EMR to EHR.\n- **2006:** MedicsRIS debuts for radiology\n- **2007:** MedicsDocAssistant achieves CCHIT certification. Reached 2,500 clients.\n- **2010:** Cloud implementations begin. Expanded to 15,000 sq ft building, 200+ employees.\n- **2013:** ICD-10 ready\n- **2014:** Stage 2 Meaningful Use certification; ICSA Labs certification\n- **2018:** MACRA/MIPS capability; EMRdirect launched\n- **2019:** Tens of thousands of users\n- **2020:** Processing nearly 50 million EDI transactions annually\n\nThis tells us MedicsDocAssistant is a **mature product with roots going back to 1996** (beta) and **2000** (full production), making it roughly 25+ years old.\n\n---\n\n### SOURCE 4: http://www.adsc.com/onc-acb-health-it-certification-program-medicsdocassistant\n\n**Source 15.02.05.1044.AVDD.01.01.1.220111\n- **Certification date:** January 11, 2022\n- **Certification type:** ONC Health IT Ambulatory EHR\n- **Certified criteria:** 52 regulatory criteria across categories (a) through (h) under 170.315\n- **Clinical Quality Measures (CQMs):** 25 CMS quality metrics covering chronic disease management (diabetes, hypertension), preventive screening (cancer, System transmits to appropriate departments, labs, pharmacies. Includes real-time clinical decision support: dosage suggestions, duplicate therapy warnings, drug-to-drug and drug-to-allergy interaction checking.\n2. **Evidence-Based Medicine:** \"Blue ribbons\" supporting provider judgment with best available evidence from systematic research.\n3. **MedicsRx e-Prescribing:** Secure electronic prescription transfer to all connected pharmacies. Supports controlled substances in eligible states.\n4. **MedicsPortal (Patient Portal):** Patient scheduling, online questionnaires, secure communication, online payments. Dynamically connects practice to patient.\n5. **Medics On-Call for Smartphone:** Point-of-care data entry via iPhone/smartphone. Access patient information during hospital rounds or off-site.\n6. **Medics FlowText:** Physicians create reports/letters using Dragon Medical transcription, handwriting recognition, or keyboard. Text automatically flows into corresponding patient record fields.\n7. **Encounter-Based Model:** Documentation created by physician during the encounter with integrated facilities for organizing patient data and accessing medical term glossaries.\n8. **Role-Based Security:** Differing levels of access customizable by role -- entering, changing, or viewing patient information by individual patient or patient groups.\n\n---\n\n### SOURCE 8: https://www.adsc.com/practice-management-software (MedicsPremier)\n\n**Source type:** Vendor product page for practice management software\n\n**Key findings -- MedicsPremier manages:**\n- **Scheduling:** Multi-modality scheduler for staff, resources, and ancillary services. Synthesis\n\n### What is ADSC?\nAdvanced Data Systems Corporation is a **privately-held, independently-owned, debt-free healthcare IT company** founded in **1977** by David Barzillai in New York City. It has **300+ employees** working from their own 15,000 sq ft building. They have never merged, been acquired, or discontinued a product. They process nearly **50 million EDI transactions annually** and serve **thousands of healthcare organizations** across dozens of specialties. They maintain all development, implementation, training, and support in-house.\n\n### What is MedicsDocAssistant?\nMedicsDocAssistant is ADSC's **legacy/older-generation ambulatory EHR**, first beta-released in **1996** and in full production since **2000**. The currently certified version is **8.0** (certified January 2022). It is a **complete ambulatory EHR** with ONC 2015 Edition Cures Update certification across **52 criteria**. Its intended users are **\"Internal Medicine and Other Specialties Providers and office staff.\"** It integrates with **Newcrop for e-prescribing** and **Surescripts for Direct messaging**. ADSC also has a **newer-generation product, MedicsCloud EHR v11.0**, which is separately certified with **56 criteria** and appears to be the go-forward platform, while MedicsDocAssistant continues to be maintained for existing customers.\n\n### Key data the system manages:\n- Patient demographics, family health history, social/psychological/behavioral data\n- Medication lists and e-prescriptions (controlled and non-controlled)\n- Lab orders and results (bidirectional with labs)\n- Diagnostic imaging orders\n- Problem lists, care plans, treatment plans\n- Implantable device lists\n- Clinical encounter documentation (template-based, voice-dictated, or typed)\n- Clinical quality measures (25 CQMs)\n- Immunization records (with registry reporting)\n- Public health reporting data (syndromic surveillance, cancer cases, electronic case reporting)\n- Patient portal data (appointments, forms, messages, payments)\n- Scheduling, billing claims, insurance eligibility, financial data (via MedicsPremier integration)\n\n### Broader product ecosystem:\n1. **MedicsDocAssistant** -- Legacy ambulatory EHR (v8.0, ONC certified)\n2. **MedicsCloud EHR** -- Current-generation ambulatory EHR Raymond Dimadian MD's radiology practice (where MRI was invented) implements the system; that practice evolved into HMCA (now 25 radiology centers)\n- **1983:** MEDICS II with floppy disk billing and automated scheduling. Reached 500 clients.\n- **1989:** Electronic claims submission via modem; IBM Business Partner\n- **1993:** MedicsElite released (GUI product). Reached 65+ employees, 1,000+ clients\n- **1996:** MedicsDocAssistant EMR limited beta release -- this is the product origin\n- **2000:** MedicsDocAssistant EHR V4 moves to full production. Transitions from EMR to EHR.\n- **2006:** MedicsRIS debuts for radiology\n- **2007:** MedicsDocAssistant achieves CCHIT certification. Reached 2,500 clients.\n- **2010:** Cloud implementations begin. Expanded to 15,000 sq ft building, 200+ employees.\n- **2013:** ICD-10 ready\n- **2014:** Stage 2 Meaningful Use certification; ICSA Labs certification\n- **2018:** MACRA/MIPS capability; EMRdirect launched\n- **2019:** Tens of thousands of users\n- **2020:** Processing nearly 50 million EDI transactions annually\n\nThis tells us MedicsDocAssistant is a **mature product with roots going back to 1996** (beta) and **2000** (full production), making it roughly 25+ years old.\n\n---\n\n### SOURCE 4: http://www.adsc.com/onc-acb-health-it-certification-program-medicsdocassistant\n\n**Source type:** ONC mandatory disclosures page (regulatory)\n\n**Key findings:**\n- **Product:** MedicsDocAssistant, Version 8.0\n- **Developer:** Advanced Data Systems Corporation\n- **Certification ID:** 15.02.05.1044.AVDD.01.01.1.220111\n- **Certification date:** January 11, 2022\n- **Certification type:** ONC Health IT Ambulatory EHR\n- **Certified criteria:** 52 regulatory criteria across categories (a) through (h) under 170.315\n- **Clinical Quality Measures (CQMs):** 25 CMS quality metrics covering chronic disease management (diabetes, hypertension), preventive screening (cancer, depression, immunization), and medication safety\n- **Integrated third-party components:** Newcrop for eRx, Surescripts N2N Direct Messaging, Meinberg NTP Daemon for NTP\n- **Cost structure:** Addresses 170.315(b)(10) -- Electronic Health Information export\n- Supports both single patient EHI export and population-level EHI export\n- Two export formats:\n  1. **HL7 CCDA XML** complying with specialties including pain management, behavioral health, podiatry, ophthalmology, orthopedics, neurology, radiology\n- **Infrastructure:** Cloud-hosted on Equinix\n\n---\n\n### SOURCE 7: https://www.adsc.com/ehr-all-features and https://www.adsc.com/ehr-software-features\n\n**Source type:** Vendor detailed EHR feature pages\n\n**Key findings -- specific MedicsDocAssistant/MedicsCloud EHR features:**\n1. **Computer-Based Provider Order Entry (CPOE):** Provider enters orders for medications, lab/radiology tests, procedures directly into patient record. System transmits to appropriate departments, labs, pharmacies. Includes real-time clinical decision support: dosage suggestions, duplicate therapy warnings, drug-to-drug and drug-to-allergy interaction checking.\n2. **Evidence-Based Medicine:** \"Blue ribbons\" supporting provider judgment with best available evidence from systematic research.\n3. **MedicsRx e-Prescribing:** Secure electronic prescription transfer to all connected pharmacies. Supports controlled substances in eligible states.\n4. **MedicsPortal (Patient Portal):** Patient scheduling, online questionnaires, secure communication, online payments. Dynamically connects practice to patient.\n5. **Medics On-Call for Smartphone:** Point-of-care data entry via iPhone/smartphone. Access patient information during hospital rounds or off-site.\n6. **Medics FlowText:** Physicians create reports/letters using Dragon Medical transcription, handwriting recognition, or keyboard. Text automatically flows into corresponding patient record fields.\n7. **Encounter-Based Model:** Documentation created by physician during the encounter with integrated facilities for organizing patient data and accessing medical term glossaries.\n8. **Role-Based Security:** Differing levels of access customizable by role -- entering, changing, or viewing patient information by individual patient or patient groups.\n\n---\n\n### SOURCE 8: https://www.adsc.com/practice-management-software (MedicsPremier)\n\n**Source type:** Vendor product page for practice management software\n\n**Key findings -- MedicsPremier manages:**\n- **Scheduling:** Multi-modality scheduler for staff, resources, and ancillary services. Synthesis\n\n### What is ADSC?\nAdvanced Data Systems Corporation is a **privately-held, independently-owned, debt-free healthcare IT company** founded in **1977** by David Barzillai in New York City. It has **300+ employees** working from their own 15,000 sq ft building. They have never merged, been acquired, or discontinued a product. They process nearly **50 million EDI transactions annually** and serve **thousands of healthcare organizations** across dozens of specialties. They maintain all development, implementation, training, and support in-house.\n\n### What is MedicsDocAssistant?\nMedicsDocAssistant is ADSC's **legacy/older-generation ambulatory EHR**, first beta-released in **1996** and in full production since **2000**. The currently certified version is **8.0** (certified January 2022). It is a **complete ambulatory EHR** with ONC 2015 Edition Cures Update certification across **52 criteria**. Its intended users are **\"Internal Medicine and Other Specialties Providers and office staff.\"** It integrates with **Newcrop for e-prescribing** and **Surescripts for Direct messaging**. ADSC also has a **newer-generation product, MedicsCloud EHR v11.0**, which is separately certified with **56 criteria** and appears to be the go-forward platform, while MedicsDocAssistant continues to be maintained for existing customers.\n\n### Key data the system manages:\n- Patient demographics, family health history, social/psychological/behavioral data\n- Medication lists and e-prescriptions (controlled and non-controlled)\n- Lab orders and results (bidirectional with labs)\n- Diagnostic imaging orders\n- Problem lists, care plans, treatment plans\n- Implantable device lists\n- Clinical encounter documentation (template-based, voice-dictated, or typed)\n- Clinical quality measures (25 CQMs)\n- Immunization records (with registry reporting)\n- Public health reporting data (syndromic surveillance, cancer cases, electronic case reporting)\n- Patient portal data (appointments, forms, messages, payments)\n- Scheduling, billing claims, insurance eligibility, financial data (via MedicsPremier integration)\n\n### Broader product ecosystem:\n1. **MedicsDocAssistant** -- Legacy ambulatory EHR (v8.0, ONC certified)\n2. **MedicsCloud EHR** -- Current-generation ambulatory EHR (v11.0, ONC certified)\n3. **MedicsPremier** -- Practice management (scheduling, billing, claims, financial analytics, CRM)\n4. **MedicsRIS** -- Radiology information system with PACS integration\n5. **MedicsScribeAI** -- AI voice-to-text clinical documentation\n6. **MedicsAI Fax** -- AI-powered fax document routing\n7. **Medics Me** -- Patient-facing mobile app (OAuth 2, Cures Act compliant)\n8. **MedicsPortal** -- Patient portal (web-based)\n9. **MedicsMobile** -- Provider mobile app\n10. **MedicsRX** -- Standalone e-prescribing module\n11. **MedicsDocAssistantOffice** -- Entry-level/tablet-based compact EHR\n12. **ADSRCM** -- Fully outsourced revenue cycle management service (300+ staff)\n\nSources:\n- [ADSC Main Website](https://www.adsc.com)\n- [ADSC About Originally created as a patient demographics database for radiology practices, the company has grown to over 300 employees operating from a 15,000 sq ft headquarters in Paramus, NJ, with additional offices in Plymouth Meeting PA, Laurel MD, and Naples FL. ADS has never been acquired, merged, or changed its name, and claims to have never discontinued a product. The company processes nearly 50 million EDI transactions annually and claims to serve \"thousands of healthcare organizations\" with 30,000–40,000+ providers. Medical Economics ranked ADS #21 among EHR vendors in 2013. All development, implementation, training, and support are handled in-house. Leadership includes founder David Barzillai (President), George Grodentzik (Partner EVP), and Sheryl Miller MBA (EVP Sales & Business Development).\n\nADS is a small-to-mid-size vendor in the ambulatory EHR market. Third-party estimates place their revenue somewhere between $17M and $46M (estimates vary widely). Enlyft data suggests roughly 0.02% healthcare IT market share, with customers skewing toward small and medium practices (70% under 1000 employees), overwhelmingly US-based (95%). The company serves over 28 clinical specialties with dedicated product configurations, with particular strength in behavioral health/addiction treatment, radiology, laboratory, and internal medicine.\n\n## Product: MedicsDocAssistant\n\nCHPL IDs: 10785 (version 8.0, certified 2022-01-11)\n\n### What It Is\n\nMedicsDocAssistant is ADS's legacy-generation ambulatory EHR, first beta-released in 1996 and in full production since 2000. The currently certified version is 8.0 (ONC 2015 Edition Cures Update, certified January 2022). It is broadly certified across 43 criteria spanning clinical data management (a)(1)-(a)(5), (a)(12), (a)(14)-(a)(15), transitions of care (b)(1)-(b)(3), clinical quality measures (c)(1)-(c)(3), patient access/portal (e)(1), (e)(3), public health reporting (f)(1)-(f)(2), (f)(4)-(f)(5), standardized APIs including FHIR (g)(2)-(g)(10), and Direct messaging (h)(1). The SED intended user description is \"Internal Medicine and Other Specialities Providers and office staff.\"\n\nMedicsDocAssistant is the EHR component of a larger product suite. It integrates tightly with **MedicsPremier** (practice management/billing), and is typically deployed alongside it as the \"All-in-One Suite.\" ADS also separately certifies a newer-generation product, **MedicsCloud EHR v11.0** (CHPL ID 15.02.05.1044.AVDC.01.01.1.220111, 56 criteria), which appears to be the current go-forward platform. MedicsDocAssistant continues to be maintained and certified for existing customers. The two products share the same vendor, similar feature sets, and the same integration ecosystem, but are separately certified. The vendor's marketing pages now largely describe \"MedicsCloud EHR\" as the current product, with Capterra explicitly noting \"MedicsCloud EHR (formerly MedicsDocAssistant EHR).\"\n\nThird-party e-prescribing is handled via **Newcrop** (for MedicsDocAssistant specifically; MedicsCloud uses Surescripts directly). Direct messaging uses **Surescripts N2N Direct Messaging**. The system also includes Meinberg NTP Daemon for time synchronization.\n\n### Users & Market\n\nMedicsDocAssistant targets ambulatory practices of all sizes, from solo providers to multi-site groups and enterprise health systems. The vendor claims over 28 specialties with dedicated configurations, including: internal medicine, family practice, behavioral health/addiction treatment, radiology, laboratory, cardiology, dermatology, ophthalmology, podiatry, orthopedics, neurology, psychiatry, OB/GYN, oncology, urology, pain management, gastroenterology, nephrology, pulmonology, ENT, general surgery, ambulatory surgery centers, genetics labs, infusion centers, infectious diseases, concierge medicine, physical therapy, and plastic surgery.\n\nNamed customers include Park Avenue Medical Professionals, Horizon Family Medical Group, Catholic Charities USA, Hispanic Counseling Center, New Bridge Medical Center, Podiatry Center of New Jersey, Health Management Corporation of America (HMCA — 25 radiology centers), University Diagnostic Medical Imaging, DON Recovery Services (addiction treatment), Transformations TMS Centers, and the Florida Bureau of Public Health Laboratories.\n\nDay-to-day users include physicians, nurses, billing staff, and practice managers. The patient portal extends access to patients. The product is cloud-hosted on Equinix infrastructure, though historical versions were on-premise.\n\nReviews are mixed — Capterra shows 3.6/5 (11 reviews), SoftwareFinder shows 3.7/5 (23 reviews), while EMRSystems.net shows 5/5 (4 reviews, small sample). FindEMR shows 50% excellent / 45% good (32 reviews). Pricing is approximately $300/month per provider.\n\n### Modules & Functionality\n\nBased on vendor product pages (adsc.com/ehr-all-features, adsc.com/electronic-health-records-software, adsc.com/ehr-software-features) and third-party reviews:\n\n**Clinical Documentation & Charting:**\n- Encounter-based documentation model — physicians create clinical notes during encounters\n- Specialty-specific customizable templates for 28+ specialties\n- FlowText: voice-command data entry using Dragon Medical transcription, handwriting recognition, or keyboard; text automatically flows into corresponding patient record fields\n- MedicsScribe AI: real-time conversational dictation/transcription with Pause & Continue, Micro Notes, Whisper X (described on vendor AI page; unclear if available in MedicsDocAssistant v8 specifically vs MedicsCloud only)\n- Patient Timeline: chronological display of all items in a patient's record (praised by Capterra reviewers)\n- Exit Plan: tracks impression/plan across encounters (praised by Capterra reviewers)\n- Evidence-Based Medicine: \"blue ribbon\" indicators supporting clinical decisions\n\n**CPOE (Computerized Provider Order Entry):**\n- Medication orders transmitted electronically to pharmacies\n- Lab and radiology test orders transmitted to labs/departments\n- Procedure orders\n- Clinical decision support: dosage suggestions, duplicate therapy warnings, drug-drug and drug-allergy interaction checking\n\n**e-Prescribing (MedicsRx):**\n- Electronic prescriptions to all connected pharmacies via Newcrop/Surescripts\n- Controlled and non-controlled substances (in eligible states)\n- Drug interaction and allergy alerts\n\n**Lab Integration:**\n- Automated lab orders with bidirectional results integration\n- Results flow back into patient records\n\n**Patient Portal (MedicsPortal):**\n- Patient scheduling\n- Online questionnaires/forms\n- Secure messaging with practice\n- Online payments\n- View/download/transmit health records (per (e)(1) certification)\n\n**Mobile Access:**\n- Medics On-Call: provider access to patient data via iPhone/smartphone for hospital rounds or off-site use\n- MedicsMobile: mobile app for patients/providers\n\n**Public Health Reporting:**\n- Immunization registry reporting (f)(1)\n- Syndromic surveillance (f)(2)\n- Cancer case reporting (f)(4)\n- Electronic case reporting (f)(5)\n\n**Clinical Quality Measures:**\n- 25 CQMs covering depression screening, diabetes management, cancer screening, immunization tracking, medication documentation, hypertension, preventive screening\n- MIPS reporting dashboard\n\n**AI Features (described on vendor AI page — may be MedicsCloud-specific):**\n- AI Rules Engine for billing/claims processing\n- HCC Coding AI for risk adjustment\n- Bill Code Analyzer\n- AI Synopsis (real-time summaries of coding, prescriptions, diagnoses)\n- AI Consult Letter Generator\n- MedicsAI Fax: identifies patient demographics from incoming faxes, extracts referral types, lab reports, payer information, categorizes and routes documents\n\n**Behavioral Health/Addiction-Specific (from adsc.com/behavioral-health-addiction-treatment):**\n- ASAM-certified assessments flowing into patient timelines\n- Auto-generated problem lists and treatment plans from assessments\n- Support for outpatient psychiatry/therapy, MAT, IOP, PHP, inpatient residential, detox, group therapy\n- Dual diagnosis program tracking\n- Group therapy session documentation with attendance management\n- Medics BedManager for facility operations\n\n**Telemedicine & Remote Monitoring:**\n- Built-in telemedicine/virtual care\n- Remote patient monitoring with trend detection and automated alerts\n- Kiosk functionality for patient check-in\n\n**Interoperability:**\n- HL7 and FHIR compliant (FHIR v4.0.1, US Core v3.1.1 per EHI export docs)\n- CCDA generation and consumption\n- Surescripts Direct messaging\n- Medics Me app: patient-mediated health record exchange via OAuth 2\n\n### Data & Content\n\nBased on certified criteria, vendor feature descriptions, reviews, and press releases, MedicsDocAssistant stores or manages:\n\n**Clinical Data (per certification criteria and feature pages):**\n- Patient demographics including race, ethnicity, preferred language, sex, DOB, sexual orientation, gender identity (a)(5)\n- Problem lists / diagnoses\n- Medication lists and prescription history\n- Allergy lists with drug-allergy interaction data\n- Family health history (a)(12)\n- Social, psychological, and behavioral data (a)(15) — including smoking status\n- Implantable device lists (a)(14)\n- Vital signs\n- Lab orders and results (bidirectional integration)\n- Diagnostic imaging orders\n- Clinical encounter notes (template-based, voice-dictated, handwritten, or typed)\n- Care plans (b)(9)\n- Immunization records (with registry reporting)\n- Clinical quality measure data (25 CQMs)\n\n**Documents & Communications:**\n- Transition of care documents — CCDA send and receive (b)(1), (b)(2)\n- Patient portal messages (secure messaging)\n- Incoming faxes (categorized and attached to patient records via AI Fax)\n- Consult letters\n- Referral documents\n\n**Patient Portal Data:**\n- Patient-submitted questionnaires/forms\n- Appointment requests\n- Payment records (portal payments)\n\n**Public Health Data:**\n- Syndromic surveillance data\n- Cancer case reports\n- Electronic case reports\n- Immunization data for registry submission\n\n**Audit & Security Data (per (d) criteria):**\n- Authentication credentials with encryption\n- Audit logs of all access/changes\n- Amendment records\n- Access control configurations\n\n**What is NOT directly in MedicsDocAssistant but in the integrated suite:**\n\nMedicsDocAssistant is the EHR component; billing, scheduling, and financial data live primarily in **MedicsPremier** (practice management), which is typically deployed alongside it. MedicsPremier manages:\n- Appointment scheduling (multi-modality)\n- Patient demographics (Master Patient Index)\n- Insurance/payer information and eligibility\n- Claims data (HCFA, UB, Workers' Comp, No-Fault formats)\n- Payment/financial transactions\n- Denial management records\n- Inventory and sales tax tracking\n- CRM/marketing campaign data\n- Financial analytics and KPIs\n\nThe vendor markets these as a tightly integrated \"All-in-One Suite\" with shared data and no redundant data entry. For EHI export purposes under (b)(10), the question is whether MedicsDocAssistant's export includes data from MedicsPremier when deployed together, or only the EHR-side data. The EHI export documentation page (apps.medicscloud.com/B10_MedicsDocAssistant.html) describes exports in CCDA XML and FHIR v4.0.1 formats — neither of which naturally carries billing claims data, suggesting PM data may not be included.\n\n**Radiology-specific data** (for practices using MedicsRIS alongside): radiology reports, imaging orders, mammography tracking, biopsy results, PACS-linked images. MedicsRIS is a separate product.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":12804,"cache_read_input_tokens":28436,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":12804},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"23f94360-d7fe-400c-8294-9ace18779e06","uuid":"b2e89b47-5526-47b5-8abc-1e32c30e9ebe"}

It is broadly certified across 43 criteria spanning clinical data management (a)(1)-(a)(5), (a)(12), (a)(14)-(a)(15), transitions of care (b)(1)-(b)(3), clinical quality measures (c)(1)-(c)(3), patient access/portal (e)(1), (e)(3), public health reporting (f)(1)-(f)(2), (f)(4)-(f)(5), standardized APIs including FHIR (g)(2)-(g)(10), and Direct messaging (h)(1). The SED intended user description is \"Internal Medicine and Other Specialities Providers and office staff.\"\n\nMedicsDocAssistant is the EHR component of a larger product suite. It integrates tightly with **MedicsPremier** (practice management/billing), and is typically deployed alongside it as the \"All-in-One Suite.\" ADS also separately certifies a newer-generation product, **MedicsCloud EHR v11.0** (CHPL ID 15.02.05.1044.AVDC.01.01.1.220111, 56 criteria), which appears to be the current go-forward platform. MedicsDocAssistant continues to be maintained and certified for existing customers. The two products share the same vendor, similar feature sets, and the same integration ecosystem, but are separately certified. The vendor's marketing pages now largely describe \"MedicsCloud EHR\" as the current product, with Capterra explicitly noting \"MedicsCloud EHR (formerly MedicsDocAssistant EHR).\"\n\nThird-party e-prescribing is handled via **Newcrop** (for MedicsDocAssistant specifically; MedicsCloud uses Surescripts directly). Direct messaging uses **Surescripts N2N Direct Messaging**. The system also includes Meinberg NTP Daemon for time synchronization.\n\n### Users & Market\n\nMedicsDocAssistant targets ambulatory practices of all sizes, from solo providers to multi-site groups and enterprise health systems. The vendor claims over 28 specialties with dedicated configurations, including: internal medicine, family practice, behavioral health/addiction treatment, radiology, laboratory, cardiology, dermatology, ophthalmology, podiatry, orthopedics, neurology, psychiatry, OB/GYN, oncology, urology, pain management, gastroenterology, nephrology, pulmonology, ENT, general surgery, ambulatory surgery centers, genetics labs, infusion centers, infectious diseases, concierge medicine, physical therapy, and plastic surgery.\n\nNamed customers include Park Avenue Medical Professionals, Horizon Family Medical Group, Catholic Charities USA, Hispanic Counseling Center, New Bridge Medical Center, Podiatry Center of New Jersey, Health Management Corporation of America (HMCA — 25 radiology centers), University Diagnostic Medical Imaging, DON Recovery Services (addiction treatment), Transformations TMS Centers, and the Florida Bureau of Public Health Laboratories.\n\nDay-to-day users include physicians, nurses, billing staff, and practice managers. The patient portal extends access to patients. The product is cloud-hosted on Equinix infrastructure, though historical versions were on-premise.\n\nReviews are mixed — Capterra shows 3.6/5 (11 reviews), SoftwareFinder shows 3.7/5 (23 reviews), while EMRSystems.net shows 5/5 (4 reviews, small sample). FindEMR shows 50% excellent / 45% good (32 reviews). Pricing is approximately $300/month per provider.\n\n### Modules & Functionality\n\nBased on vendor product pages (adsc.com/ehr-all-features, adsc.com/electronic-health-records-software, adsc.com/ehr-software-features) and third-party reviews:\n\n**Clinical Documentation & Charting:**\n- Encounter-based documentation model — physicians create clinical notes during encounters\n- Specialty-specific customizable templates for 28+ specialties\n- FlowText: voice-command data entry using Dragon Medical transcription, handwriting recognition, or keyboard; text automatically flows into corresponding patient record fields\n- MedicsScribe AI: real-time conversational dictation/transcription with Pause & Continue, Micro Notes, Whisper X (described on vendor AI page; unclear if available in MedicsDocAssistant v8 specifically vs MedicsCloud only)\n- Patient Timeline: chronological display of all items in a patient's record (praised by Capterra reviewers)\n- Exit Plan: tracks impression/plan across encounters (praised by Capterra reviewers)\n- Evidence-Based Medicine: \"blue ribbon\" indicators supporting clinical decisions\n\n**CPOE (Computerized Provider Order Entry):**\n- Medication orders transmitted electronically to pharmacies\n- Lab and radiology test orders transmitted to labs/departments\n- Procedure orders\n- Clinical decision support: dosage suggestions, duplicate therapy warnings, drug-drug and drug-allergy interaction checking\n\n**e-Prescribing (MedicsRx):**\n- Electronic prescriptions to all connected pharmacies via Newcrop/Surescripts\n- Controlled and non-controlled substances (in eligible states)\n- Drug interaction and allergy alerts\n\n**Lab Integration:**\n- Automated lab orders with bidirectional results integration\n- Results flow back into patient records\n\n**Patient Portal (MedicsPortal):**\n- Patient scheduling\n- Online questionnaires/forms\n- Secure messaging with practice\n- Online payments\n- View/download/transmit health records (per (e)(1) certification)\n\n**Mobile Access:**\n- Medics On-Call: provider access to patient data via iPhone/smartphone for hospital rounds or off-site use\n- MedicsMobile: mobile app for patients/providers\n\n**Public Health Reporting:**\n- Immunization registry reporting (f)(1)\n- Syndromic surveillance (f)(2)\n- Cancer case reporting (f)(4)\n- Electronic case reporting (f)(5)\n\n**Clinical Quality Measures:**\n- 25 CQMs covering depression screening, diabetes management, cancer screening, immunization tracking, medication documentation, hypertension, preventive screening\n- MIPS reporting dashboard\n\n**AI Features (described on vendor AI page — may be MedicsCloud-specific):**\n- AI Rules Engine for billing/claims processing\n- HCC Coding AI for risk adjustment\n- Bill Code Analyzer\n- AI Synopsis (real-time summaries of coding, prescriptions, diagnoses)\n- AI Consult Letter Generator\n- MedicsAI Fax: identifies patient demographics from incoming faxes, extracts referral types, lab reports, payer information, categorizes and routes documents\n\n**Behavioral Health/Addiction-Specific (from adsc.com/behavioral-health-addiction-treatment):**\n- ASAM-certified assessments flowing into patient timelines\n- Auto-generated problem lists and treatment plans from assessments\n- Support for outpatient psychiatry/therapy, MAT, IOP, PHP, inpatient residential, detox, group therapy\n- Dual diagnosis program tracking\n- Group therapy session documentation with attendance management\n- Medics BedManager for facility operations\n\n**Telemedicine & Remote Monitoring:**\n- Built-in telemedicine/virtual care\n- Remote patient monitoring with trend detection and automated alerts\n- Kiosk functionality for patient check-in\n\n**Interoperability:**\n- HL7 and FHIR compliant (FHIR v4.0.1, US Core v3.1.1 per EHI export docs)\n- CCDA generation and consumption\n- Surescripts Direct messaging\n- Medics Me app: patient-mediated health record exchange via OAuth 2\n\n### Data & Content\n\nBased on certified criteria, vendor feature descriptions, reviews, and press releases, MedicsDocAssistant stores or manages:\n\n**Clinical Data (per certification criteria and feature pages):**\n- Patient demographics including race, ethnicity, preferred language, sex, DOB, sexual orientation, gender identity (a)(5)\n- Problem lists / diagnoses\n- Medication lists and prescription history\n- Allergy lists with drug-allergy interaction data\n- Family health history (a)(12)\n- Social, psychological, and behavioral data (a)(15) — including smoking status\n- Implantable device lists (a)(14)\n- Vital signs\n- Lab orders and results (bidirectional integration)\n- Diagnostic imaging orders\n- Clinical encounter notes (template-based, voice-dictated, handwritten, or typed)\n- Care plans (b)(9)\n- Immunization records (with registry reporting)\n- Clinical quality measure data (25 CQMs)\n\n**Documents & Communications:**\n- Transition of care documents — CCDA send and receive (b)(1), (b)(2)\n- Patient portal messages (secure messaging)\n- Incoming faxes (categorized and attached to patient records via AI Fax)\n- Consult letters\n- Referral documents\n\n**Patient Portal Data:**\n- Patient-submitted questionnaires/forms\n- Appointment requests\n- Payment records (portal payments)\n\n**Public Health Data:**\n- Syndromic surveillance data\n- Cancer case reports\n- Electronic case reports\n- Immunization data for registry submission\n\n**Audit & Security Data (per (d) criteria):**\n- Authentication credentials with encryption\n- Audit logs of all access/changes\n- Amendment records\n- Access control configurations\n\n**What is NOT directly in MedicsDocAssistant but in the integrated suite:**\n\nMedicsDocAssistant is the EHR component; billing, scheduling, and financial data live primarily in **MedicsPremier** (practice management), which is typically deployed alongside it. MedicsPremier manages:\n- Appointment scheduling (multi-modality)\n- Patient demographics (Master Patient Index)\n- Insurance/payer information and eligibility\n- Claims data (HCFA, UB, Workers' Comp, No-Fault formats)\n- Payment/financial transactions\n- Denial management records\n- Inventory and sales tax tracking\n- CRM/marketing campaign data\n- Financial analytics and KPIs\n\nThe vendor markets these as a tightly integrated \"All-in-One Suite\" with shared data and no redundant data entry. For EHI export purposes under (b)(10), the question is whether MedicsDocAssistant's export includes data from MedicsPremier when deployed together, or only the EHR-side data. The EHI export documentation page (apps.medicscloud.com/B10_MedicsDocAssistant.html) describes exports in CCDA XML and FHIR v4.0.1 formats — neither of which naturally carries billing claims data, suggesting PM data may not be included.\n\n**Radiology-specific data** (for practices using MedicsRIS alongside): radiology reports, imaging orders, mammography tracking, biopsy results, PACS-linked images. MedicsRIS is a separate product.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
